$ sed -n 1,400p impl/FeedHandler.java | nl -ba
     1	package com.ernieyu.feedparser.impl;
     2	
     3	import java.util.Stack;
     4	
     5	import org.apache.commons.text.StringEscapeUtils;
     6	import org.xml.sax.Attributes;
     7	import org.xml.sax.SAXException;
     8	import org.xml.sax.helpers.DefaultHandler;
     9	
    10	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Feed;
    11	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.FeedType;
    12	
    13	/**
    14	 * SAX parser content handler to process feed XML.
    15	 */
    16	class FeedHandler extends DefaultHandler {
    17	    private static final String RDF = "rdf";
    18	    private static final String RSS = "rss";
    19	    private static final String FEED = "feed";
    20	    private static final String ITEM = "item";
    21	    private static final String ENTRY = "entry";
    22	
    23	    private Stack<BaseElement> elementStack;
    24	    private Feed feed;
    25	    private FeedType type;
    26	    private StringBuilder buffer;
    27	    
    28	    /**
    29	     * Returns the feed.
    30	     */
    31	    public Feed getFeed() {
    32	        return feed;
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    @Override
    36	    public void characters(char[] ch, int start, int length) throws SAXException {
    37	        super.characters(ch, start, length);
    38	        buffer.append(ch, start, length);
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    @Override
    42	    public void startDocument() throws SAXException {
    43	        super.startDocument();
    44	        elementStack = new Stack<BaseElement>();
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    @Override
    48	    public void endDocument() throws SAXException {
    49	        elementStack.clear();
    50	        super.endDocument();
    51	    }
    52	    
    53	    @Override
    54	    public void startElement(String uri, String localName, String qName,
    55	            Attributes attributes) throws SAXException {
    56	        super.startElement(uri, localName, qName, attributes);
    57	        
    58	        BaseElement newElement;
    59	        
    60	        if (RDF.equalsIgnoreCase(localName)) {
    61	            // Create feed for RSS 1.0.
    62	            Rss1Feed newFeed = new Rss1Feed(uri, RDF, attributes);
    63	            feed = newFeed;
    64	            type = FeedType.RSS_1_0;
    65	            newElement = newFeed;
    66	            
    67	        } else if (RSS.equalsIgnoreCase(localName)) {
    68	            // Create feed for RSS 2.0.
    69	            Rss2Feed newFeed = new Rss2Feed(uri, RSS, attributes);
    70	            feed = newFeed;
    71	            type = FeedType.RSS_2_0;
    72	            newElement = newFeed;
    73	            
    74	        } else if (FEED.equalsIgnoreCase(localName)) {
    75	            // Create feed for Atom 1.0.
    76	            AtomFeed newFeed = new AtomFeed(uri, FEED, attributes);
    77	            feed = newFeed;
    78	            type = FeedType.ATOM_1_0;
    79	            newElement = newFeed;
    80	            
    81	        } else if (ITEM.equalsIgnoreCase(localName)) {
    82	            // Create RSS item.
    83	            switch (type) {
    84	            case RSS_1_0:
    85	                newElement = new Rss1Item(uri, localName, attributes);
    86	                break;
    87	            case RSS_2_0:
    88	                newElement = new Rss2Item(uri, localName, attributes);
    89	                break;
    90	            default:
    91	                throw new SAXException("Unknown feed type");
    92	            }
    93	
    94	        } else if (ENTRY.equalsIgnoreCase(localName)) {
    95	            // Create Atom item.
    96	            newElement = new AtomItem(uri, localName, attributes);
    97	
    98	        } else {
    99	            // Create new XML element.
   100	            newElement = new BaseElement(uri, localName, attributes);
   101	        }
   102	
   103	        elementStack.push(newElement);
   104	        
   105	        // Initialize content buffer.
   106	        buffer = new StringBuilder();
   107	    }
   108	
   109	    @Override
   110	    public void endElement(String uri, String localName, String qName)
   111	            throws SAXException {
   112	        super.endElement(uri, localName, qName);
   113	        
   114	        // Save content in current element.
   115	        BaseElement currentElement = elementStack.pop();
   116	        String content = buffer.toString();
   117	        // If contains ampersand, unescape escaped symbols
   118	        if (content.contains("&")) {
   119	            content = StringEscapeUtils.unescapeXml(content);
   120	        }
   121	        currentElement.setContent(content);
   122	        
   123	        // Add current element to its parent.
   124	        if (!elementStack.empty()) {
   125	            BaseElement parent = elementStack.peek();
   126	            parent.addElement(localName, currentElement);
   127	        }
   128	
   129	        // Clear content buffer.
   130	        buffer.delete(0, buffer.length());
   131	    }
   132	}
$ sed -n 1,400p impl/DefaultFeedParser.java | nl -ba
     1	package com.ernieyu.feedparser.impl;
     2	
     3	import java.io.InputStream;
     4	
     5	import javax.xml.parsers.SAXParser;
     6	import javax.xml.parsers.SAXParserFactory;
     7	
     8	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.XMLInputStream;
     9	import org.xml.sax.XMLReader;
    10	
    11	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Feed;
    12	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.FeedException;
    13	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.FeedParser;
    14	
    15	/**
    16	 * Default implementation of FeedParser.  This uses a SAX parser to process
    17	 * the feed.
    18	 */
    19	public class DefaultFeedParser implements FeedParser {
    20	
    21	    @Override
    22	    public Feed parse(InputStream inStream) throws FeedException {
    23	        try {
    24	            // Create SAX parser.
    25	            SAXParserFactory factory = SAXParserFactory.newInstance();
    26	            SAXParser parser = factory.newSAXParser();
    27	            
    28	            // Turn on namespace support.
    29	            XMLReader reader = parser.getXMLReader();
    30	            reader.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/namespaces", true);
    31	            reader.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/namespace-prefixes", false);
    32	            
    33	        	// Create SAX handler.
    34	        	FeedHandler handler = new FeedHandler();
    35	        	
    36	            // Parse feed and return data.
    37	        	parser.parse(new XMLInputStream(inStream), handler);
    38	        	Feed feed = handler.getFeed();
    39	            if (feed == null)
    40	                throw new FeedException("Invalid RSS/Atom feed");
    41	            
    42	            return feed;
    43	        
    44	        } catch (Exception ex) {
    45	        	throw new FeedException(ex);
    46	        }
    47	    }
    48	}
$ sed -n 1,400p impl/BaseElement.java | nl -ba
     1	package com.ernieyu.feedparser.impl;
     2	
     3	import java.util.ArrayList;
     4	import java.util.Collections;
     5	import java.util.HashMap;
     6	import java.util.List;
     7	import java.util.Map;
     8	import java.util.Set;
     9	
    10	import org.xml.sax.Attributes;
    11	import org.xml.sax.helpers.AttributesImpl;
    12	
    13	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Element;
    14	
    15	/**
    16	 * Base class for XML elements.
    17	 */
    18	class BaseElement implements Element {
    19	
    20	    private final String uri;
    21	    private final String name;
    22	    private final Attributes attributes;
    23	    private final Map<String, List<Element>> elementMap = new HashMap<String, List<Element>>();
    24	    private String content;
    25	    
    26	    /**
    27	     * Constructs an Element with the specified namespace uri and name.
    28	     */
    29	    public BaseElement(String uri, String name) {
    30	        this.uri = uri;
    31	        this.name = name;
    32	        this.attributes = new AttributesImpl();
    33	    }
    34	    
    35	    /**
    36	     * Constructs an Element with the specified namespace uri, name and 
    37	     * attributes.
    38	     */
    39	    public BaseElement(String uri, String name, Attributes attributes) {
    40	        this.uri = uri;
    41	        this.name = name;
    42	        this.attributes = new AttributesImpl(attributes);
    43	    }
    44	    
    45	    @Override
    46	    public String getUri() {
    47	        return uri;
    48	    }
    49	    
    50	    @Override
    51	    public String getName() {
    52	        return name;
    53	    }
    54	    
    55	    @Override
    56	    public Attributes getAttributes() {
    57	        return attributes;
    58	    }
    59	    
    60	    @Override
    61	    public String getContent() {
    62	        return content;
    63	    }
    64	    
    65	    /**
    66	     * Sets the element content.
    67	     */
    68	    void setContent(String data) {
    69	        this.content = data;
    70	    }
    71	    
    72	    @Override
    73	    public Element getElement(String name) {
    74	        List<Element> elements = elementMap.get(name);
    75	        return (elements != null) ? elements.get(0) : null;
    76	    }
    77	    
    78	    @Override
    79	    public List<Element> getElementList(String name) {
    80	        return elementMap.getOrDefault(name, Collections.emptyList());
    81	    }
    82	
    83	    @Override
    84	    public Set<String> getElementKeys() {
    85	        return elementMap.keySet();
    86	    }
    87	    
    88	    /**
    89	     * Adds the specified child element to the element.
    90	     */
    91	    void addElement(String name, Element element) {
    92	        if (elementMap.containsKey(name)) {
    93	            elementMap.get(name).add(element);
    94	        } else {
    95	            List<Element> newList = new ArrayList<Element>();
    96	            newList.add(element);
    97	            elementMap.put(name, newList);
    98	        }
    99	    }
   100	}

     1	package com.ernieyu.feedparser.impl;
     2	
     3	import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
     4	
     5	import org.xml.sax.Attributes;
     6	
     7	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.FeedUtils;
     8	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Item;
     9	
    10	import java.util.List;
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * Base class for feed items.
    14	 */
    15	abstract class BaseItem extends BaseElement implements Item {
    16	
    17	    /**
    18	     * Constructs a BaseItem with the specified namespace uri, name and 
    19	     * attributes.
    20	     */
    21	    public BaseItem(String uri, String name, Attributes attributes) {
    22	        super(uri, name, attributes);
    23	    }
    24	    
    25	    /**
    26	     * Returns the unique identifier for item equality.  This is usually based
    27	     * on the Guid value.  This method uses alternate values when the Guid 
    28	     * value is missing.
    29	     */
    30	    private String getUniqueId() {
    31	        String id = getGuid();
    32	        if (id == null) {
    33	            List<String> links = getLinks();
    34	            if (links.size() == 1)
    35	                id = links.get(0);
    36	        }
    37	        if (id == null) {
    38	            id = getTitle();
    39	        }
    40	        if (id == null) {
    41	            id = getDescription();
    42	        }
    43	        return id;
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    @NonNull
    47	    @Override
    48	    public String toString() {
    49	        return getTitle();
    50	    }
    51	    
    52	    @Override
    53	    public boolean equals(Object obj) {
    54	        // Compare ids for equality.
    55	        if (obj instanceof BaseItem) {
    56	            String id1 = getUniqueId();
    57	            String id2 = ((BaseItem) obj).getUniqueId();
    58	            return FeedUtils.equalsOrNull(id1, id2);
    59	        }
    60	        
    61	        return false;
    62	    }
    63	    
    64	    @Override
    65	    public int hashCode() {
    66	        // Use same fields as equals method. 
    67	        String id = getUniqueId();
    68	        return (id != null) ? id.hashCode() : 0;
    69	    }
    70	}

$ sed -n 1,400p impl/AtomItem.java | nl -ba
     1	package com.ernieyu.feedparser.impl;
     2	
     3	import java.util.ArrayList;
     4	import java.util.Date;
     5	import java.util.List;
     6	
     7	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.*;
     8	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.MediaRss;
     9	import org.xml.sax.Attributes;
    10	
    11	/**
    12	 * Item implementation for Atom feeds.
    13	 */
    14	class AtomItem extends BaseItem {
    15	    // XML elements for Atom items.
    16	    private static final String TITLE = "title";
    17	    private static final String LINK = "link";
    18	    private static final String UPDATED = "updated";
    19	    private static final String ID = "id";
    20	    private static final String CONTENT = "content";
    21	    private static final String SUMMARY = "summary";
    22	    private static final String AUTHOR = "author";
    23	    private static final String NAME = "name";
    24	    private static final String CATEGORY = "category";
    25	    
    26	    /**
    27	     * Constructs an AtomItem with the specified namespace uri, name and 
    28	     * attributes.
    29	     */
    30	    public AtomItem(String uri, String name, Attributes attributes) {
    31	        super(uri, name, attributes);
    32	    }
    33	    
    34	    @Override
    35	    public FeedType getType() {
    36	        return FeedType.ATOM_1_0;
    37	    }
    38	    
    39	    @Override
    40	    public String getTitle() {
    41	        Element title = getElement(TITLE);
    42	        return (title != null) ? title.getContent() : null;
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    @Override
    46	    public List<String> getLinks() {
    47	        ArrayList<String> links = new ArrayList<String>();
    48	        List<Element> elements = getElementList(LINK);
    49	        for (Element element : elements) {
    50	            links.add(element.getAttributes().getValue("href"));
    51	        }
    52	
    53	        return links;
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    @Override
    57	    public String getDescription() {
    58	        Element descr = getElement(CONTENT);
    59	        if (descr == null) descr = getElement(SUMMARY);
    60	        return (descr != null) ? descr.getContent() : null;
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    @Override
    64	    public String getAuthor() {
    65	        Element author = getElement(AUTHOR);
    66	        if (author != null) {
    67	            Element name = author.getElement(NAME);
    68	            return (name != null) ? name.getContent() : null;
    69	        }
    70	        return null;
    71	    }
    72	
    73	    @Override
    74	    public String getGuid() {
    75	        Element guid = getElement(ID);
    76	        return (guid != null) ? guid.getContent() : null;
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    @Override
    80	    public Date getPubDate() {
    81	        Element pubDate = getElement(UPDATED);
    82	        return (pubDate != null) ? FeedUtils.convertAtomDate(pubDate.getContent()) : null;
    83	    }
    84	
    85	    @Override
    86	    public List<String> getCategories() {
    87	        List<Element> elementList = getElementList(CATEGORY);
    88	        
    89	        // Create list of category terms.
    90	        List<String> categories = new ArrayList<String>();
    91	        for (Element element : elementList) {
    92	            categories.add(element.getAttributes().getValue("term"));
    93	        }
    94	        
    95	        return categories;
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    @Override
    99	    public List<Enclosure> getEnclosures() {
   100	        ArrayList<Enclosure> enclosures = new ArrayList<Enclosure>();
   101	        List<Element> links = getElementList(LINK);
   102	
   103	        for (Element link : links) {
   104	            Attributes attr = link.getAttributes();
   105	            String rel = attr.getValue("rel");
   106	            if (rel == null || !rel.equalsIgnoreCase("enclosure"))
   107	                continue;
   108	
   109	            String url = attr.getValue("href");
   110	            String type = attr.getValue("type");
   111	            String lengthStr = attr.getValue("length");
   112	            long length = 0;
   113	            if (lengthStr != null)
   114	                length = Long.parseLong(lengthStr);
   115	
   116	            enclosures.add(new Enclosure(url, type, length));
   117	        }
   118	
   119	        return enclosures;
   120	    }
   121	
   122	    /**
   123	     * Not supported
   124	     */
   125	
   126	    @Override
   127	    public MediaRss getMediaRss() {
   128	        return null;
   129	    }
   130	
   131	    /**
   132	     * Not supported
   133	     */
   134	
   135	    @Override
   136	    public EzRssTorrentItem getEzRssTorrentItem() {
   137	        return null;
   138	    }
   139	}

    20	package org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core;
    21	
    22	import android.content.Context;
    23	import android.text.TextUtils;
    24	
    25	import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
    26	
    27	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Enclosure;
    28	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.EzRssTorrentItem;
    29	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Feed;
    30	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.FeedParserFactory;
    31	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Item;
    32	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.Content;
    33	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.Hash;
    34	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.MediaRss;
    35	
    36	import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core.model.data.entity.FeedChannel;
    37	import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core.model.data.entity.FeedItem;
    38	import org.proninyaroslav.libretorrent.core.utils.Utils;
    39	
    40	import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
    41	import java.util.ArrayList;
    42	import java.util.Date;
    43	import java.util.List;
    44	
    45	/*
    46	 * Parser RSS/Atom feeds.
    47	 *
    48	 * Tags are supported for items:
    49	 *  - <link>
    50	 *  - <enclosure>
    51	 *  - <media:content>
    52	 *  - <media:hash>
    53	 *  - <guid> (sometimes the torrent link is encoded as the GUID in RSS feeds)
    54	 *  - ezRSS <torrent: ... > namespace
    55	 */
    56	
    57	public class FeedParser
    58	{
    59	    private FeedChannel feedChannel;
    60	    private Feed feed;
    61	
    62	    public FeedParser(@NonNull Context context, @NonNull FeedChannel feedChannel) throws Exception
    63	    {
    64	        this.feedChannel = feedChannel;
    65	        ByteArrayInputStream bsStream = null;
    66	        try {
    67	            byte[] response = Utils.fetchHttpUrl(context, feedChannel.url);
    68	            if (response == null)
    69	                return;
    70	            bsStream = new ByteArrayInputStream(response);
    71	            feed = FeedParserFactory.newParser().parse(bsStream);
    72	
    73	        } finally {
    74	            if (bsStream != null) {
    75	                try {
    76	                    bsStream.close();
    77	                } catch (Exception e) {
    78	                    /* Ignore */
    79	                }
    80	            }
    81	        }
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    public String getTitle()
    85	    {
    86	        return feed.getTitle();
    87	    }
    88	
    89	    public List<FeedItem> getItems()
    90	    {
    91	        List<FeedItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
    92	        if (feed == null)
    93	            return items;
    94	
    95	        for (Item item : feed.getItemList()) {
    96	            List<String> links = item.getLinks();
    97	            String articleUrl = getFirstNotNullLink(links);
    98	            String downloadUrl = watchDownloadableLink(links);
    99	
   100	            /* Find url with torrent/magnet */
   101	            if (downloadUrl == null)
   102	                downloadUrl = findDownloadUrl(item);
   103	
   104	            Date pubDate = item.getPubDate();
   105	            long pubDateTime = 0;
   106	            if (pubDate != null)
   107	                pubDateTime = pubDate.getTime();
   108	
   109	            FeedItem feedItem = new FeedItem(feedChannel.id, downloadUrl,
   110	                    articleUrl, item.getTitle(), pubDateTime);
   111	            feedItem.fetchDate = System.currentTimeMillis();
   112	            items.add(feedItem);
   113	        }
   114	
   115	        return items;
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    private String getFirstNotNullLink(List<String> links)
   119	    {
   120	        for (String link : links) {
   121	            if (!TextUtils.isEmpty(link))
   122	                return link;
   123	        }
   124	
   125	        return null;
   126	    }
   127	
   128	    private String watchDownloadableLink(List<String> links)
   129	    {
   130	        for (String link : links) {
   131	            if (link == null)
   132	                continue;
   133	
   134	            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(link))
   135	                return link;
   136	        }
   137	
   138	        return null;
   139	    }
   140	
   141	    private String findDownloadUrl(Item item)
   142	    {
   143	        /*
   144	         * Watch tags in descending order of importance
   145	         * (hashes in the last place)
   146	         */
   147	        String enclosureUrl = watchEnclosure(item);
   148	        if (enclosureUrl != null)
   149	            return enclosureUrl;
   150	
   151	        String infoHash = watchTorrentInfoHash(item);
   152	        if (infoHash != null)
   153	            return infoHash;
   154	
   155	        String mediaContentUrl = watchMediaContent(item);
   156	        if (mediaContentUrl != null)
   157	            return mediaContentUrl;
   158	
   159	        String guid = watchGuid(item);
   160	        return guid;
   161	    }
   162	
   163	    /*
   164	     * Parse element like this:
   165	     * <enclosure type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
   166	     */
   167	
   168	    private String watchEnclosure(Item item)
   169	    {
   170	        for (Enclosure enclosure : item.getEnclosures()) {
   171	            if (enclosure == null)
   172	                continue;
   173	
   174	            String url = enclosure.getUrl();
   175	            String type = enclosure.getType();
   176	            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
   177	                return url;
   178	        }
   179	
   180	        return null;
   181	    }
   182	
   183	    /*
   184	     * Parse element like this:
   185	     * <media:content type="application/x-bittorrent" url="http://foo.com/bar.torrent"/>
   186	     */
   187	
   188	    private String watchMediaContent(Item item)
   189	    {
   190	        MediaRss media = item.getMediaRss();
   191	        if (media == null)
   192	            return null;
   193	
   194	        List<Content> contentList = media.getContent();
   195	        for (Content content : contentList) {
   196	            if (content == null)
   197	                continue;
   198	
   199	            String url = content.getUrl();
   200	            if (url == null)
   201	                return watchMediaHash(media);
   202	            String type = content.getType();
   203	            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
   204	                return url;
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        return watchMediaHash(media);
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    /*
   211	     * Parse element like this:
   212	     * <media:hash algo="sha1">8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</media:hash>
   213	     */
   214	
   215	    private String watchMediaHash(MediaRss media)
   216	    {
   217	        Hash hash = media.getHash();
   218	        if (hash == null)
   219	            return null;
   220	
   221	        String hashStr = hash.getValue();
   222	        if (hashStr == null)
   223	            return null;
   224	        String algo = hash.getAlgorithm();
   225	        if (Utils.isHash(hashStr) && algo != null && algo.equalsIgnoreCase("sha1"))
   226	            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(hashStr);
   227	
   228	        return null;
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    /*
   232	     * Parse element like this:
   233	     * <torrent:infoHash>8c056e06fbc16d2a2be79cefbf3e4ddc15396abe</torrent:infoHash>
   234	     */
   235	
   236	    private String watchTorrentInfoHash(Item item)
   237	    {
   238	        EzRssTorrentItem torrentItem = item.getEzRssTorrentItem();
   239	        if (torrentItem == null)
   240	            return null;
   241	
   242	        String infoHash = torrentItem.getInfoHash();
   243	        if (infoHash != null && Utils.isHash(infoHash))
   244	            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(infoHash);
   245	
   246	        return null;
   247	    }
   248	
   249	    /*
   250	     * Parse element like this:
   251	     * <guid>http://foo.com/bar.torrent</guid>
   252	     */
   253	
   254	    private String watchGuid(Item item)
   255	    {
   256	        String url = item.getGuid();
   257	        if (url != null && isMagnetOrTorrent(url))
   258	            return url;
   259	
   260	        return null;
   261	    }
   262	
   263	    private boolean isMagnetOrTorrent(String url)
   264	    {
   265	        return url.endsWith(".torrent") || url.startsWith(Utils.MAGNET_PREFIX);
   266	    }
   267	}
$ sed -n 1,400p impl/EzRss01Parser.java | nl -ba
     1	package com.ernieyu.feedparser.impl;
     2	
     3	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Element;
     4	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.EzRssTorrentItem;
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * A parser for EzRSS 0.1 namespace (http://xmlns.ezrss.it/0.1)
     8	 */
     9	
    10	class EzRss01Parser {
    11	    private static final String EZRSS_NAMESPACE_URI = "http://xmlns.ezrss.it/0.1";
    12	    private static final String EZRSS_FILE_NAME = "fileName";
    13	    private static final String EZRSS_MAGNET_URI = "magnetURI";
    14	    private static final String EZRSS_INFO_HASH = "infoHash";
    15	    private static final String EZRSS_CONTENT_LENGTH = "contentLength";
    16	    private static final String EZRSS_SEEDS = "seeds";
    17	    private static final String EZRSS_PEERS = "peers";
    18	    private static final String EZRSS_VERIFIED = "verified";
    19	
    20	    private Rss2Item item;
    21	
    22	    public EzRss01Parser(Rss2Item item) {
    23	        this.item = item;
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    public EzRssTorrentItem parse() {
    27	        String fileName = parseFileName();
    28	        String magnetUri = parseMagnetUri();
    29	        String infoHash = parseInfoHash();
    30	        long contentLength = parseContentLength();
    31	        int seeds = parseSeeds();
    32	        int peers = parsePeers();
    33	        boolean verified = parseVerified();
    34	
    35	        return new EzRssTorrentItem(fileName, magnetUri, infoHash,
    36	            contentLength, seeds, peers, verified);
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    private String parseFileName() {
    40	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_FILE_NAME);
    41	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    42	            return null;
    43	
    44	        return element.getContent();
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    private String parseMagnetUri() {
    48	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_MAGNET_URI);
    49	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    50	            return null;
    51	
    52	        return element.getContent();
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    private String parseInfoHash() {
    56	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_INFO_HASH);
    57	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    58	            return null;
    59	
    60	        return element.getContent();
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    private long parseContentLength() {
    64	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_CONTENT_LENGTH);
    65	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    66	            return 0;
    67	
    68	        long contentLength = 0;
    69	        try {
    70	            contentLength = Long.parseLong(element.getContent());
    71	
    72	        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
    73	            return contentLength;
    74	        }
    75	
    76	        return contentLength;
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    private int parseSeeds() {
    80	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_SEEDS);
    81	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    82	            return 0;
    83	
    84	        return parseInt(element.getContent());
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    private int parsePeers() {
    88	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_PEERS);
    89	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    90	            return 0;
    91	
    92	        return parseInt(element.getContent());
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    private boolean parseVerified() {
    96	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_VERIFIED);
    97	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    98	            return false;
    99	
   100	        int verifiedVal = parseInt(element.getContent());
   101	
   102	        return verifiedVal == 1;
   103	    }
   104	
   105	    private int parseInt(String s)
   106	    {
   107	        int i = 0;
   108	        try {
   109	            i = Integer.parseInt(s);
   110	
   111	        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
   112	            return i;
   113	        }
   114	
   115	        return i;
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    private boolean isEzRssElement(Element element) {
   119	        return element.getUri() != null &&
   120	            element.getUri().startsWith(EZRSS_NAMESPACE_URI);
   121	    }
   122	}
$ sed -n 1,400p impl/MediaRssParser.java | nl -ba
     1	package com.ernieyu.feedparser.impl;
     2	
     3	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Element;
     4	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.Content;
     5	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.Hash;
     6	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.MediaRss;
     7	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.mediarss.PeerLink;
     8	import org.xml.sax.Attributes;
     9	
    10	import java.util.ArrayList;
    11	import java.util.List;
    12	
    13	/**
    14	 * A parser for Media RSS namespace (http://search.yahoo.com/mrss)
    15	 */
    16	
    17	class MediaRssParser {
    18	    private static final String MEDIA_RSS_NAMESPACE_URI = "http://search.yahoo.com/mrss";
    19	    private static final String MEDIA_RSS_CONTENT = "content";
    20	    private static final String MEDIA_RSS_HASH = "hash";
    21	    private static final String MEDIA_RSS_PEER_LINK = "peerLink";
    22	
    23	    private Rss2Item item;
    24	
    25	    public MediaRssParser(Rss2Item item) {
    26	        this.item = item;
    27	    }
    28	
    29	    public MediaRss parse()
    30	    {
    31	        MediaRssParser parser = new MediaRssParser(item);
    32	
    33	        List<Content> content = parser.parseContent();
    34	        Hash hash = parser.parseHash();
    35	        List<PeerLink> peerLinks = parser.parsePeerLinks();
    36	
    37	        return new MediaRss(content, hash, peerLinks);
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    public List<Content> parseContent() {
    41	        ArrayList<Content> content = new ArrayList<Content>();
    42	        List<Element> mediaElems = item.getElementList(MEDIA_RSS_CONTENT);
    43	
    44	        for (Element mediaElement : mediaElems) {
    45	            if (!isMediaRssElement(mediaElement))
    46	                continue;
    47	
    48	            Attributes attr = mediaElement.getAttributes();
    49	            String url = attr.getValue("url");
    50	            String type = attr.getValue("type");
    51	
    52	            content.add(new Content(url, type));
    53	        }
    54	
    55	        return content;
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    public Hash parseHash() {
    59	        Element mediaElement = item.getElement(MEDIA_RSS_HASH);
    60	        if (mediaElement == null || !isMediaRssElement(mediaElement))
    61	            return null;
    62	
    63	        Attributes attr = mediaElement.getAttributes();
    64	        String hash = mediaElement.getContent();
    65	        if (hash == null)
    66	            return null;
    67	
    68	        Hash mediaHash = new Hash(hash);
    69	        String algo = attr.getValue("algo");
    70	        if (algo != null)
    71	            mediaHash.setAlgorithm(algo);
    72	
    73	        return mediaHash;
    74	    }
    75	
    76	    public List<PeerLink> parsePeerLinks() {
    77	        ArrayList<PeerLink> peerLinks = new ArrayList<PeerLink>();
    78	        List<Element> mediaElems = item.getElementList(MEDIA_RSS_PEER_LINK);
    79	
    80	        for (Element mediaElement : mediaElems) {
    81	            if (!isMediaRssElement(mediaElement))
    82	                continue;
    83	
    84	            Attributes attr = mediaElement.getAttributes();
    85	            String href = attr.getValue("href");
    86	            String type = attr.getValue("type");
    87	
    88	            peerLinks.add(new PeerLink(href, type));
    89	        }
    90	
    91	        return peerLinks;
    92	    }
    93	
    94	    private boolean isMediaRssElement(Element element) {
    95	        return element.getUri() != null &&
    96	            element.getUri().startsWith(MEDIA_RSS_NAMESPACE_URI);
    97	    }
    98	}

    36		}

    40			return FeedType.ATOM_1_0;
    41		}

    46		    return (title != null) ? title.getContent() : null;
    47		}
    48		
    49	    @Override
    50		public String getLink() {

    52	        return (link != null) ? link.getAttributes().getValue("href") : null;
    53		}
    54		
    55	    @Override
    56		public String getDescription() {
    57	        Element descr = getElement(SUB_TITLE);
    58	        return (descr != null) ? descr.getContent() : null;
    59		}
    60	
    61	    @Override
    62	    public String getLanguage() {
    63	        // Not implemented.  Atom language is specified using the xml:lang
    64	        // attribute on elements.
    65	        return null;
    66	    }
    67	    
    68	    @Override
    69	    public String getCopyright() {
    70	        Element rights = getElement(RIGHTS);
    71	        return (rights != null) ? rights.getContent() : null;
    72	    }
    73	    
    74	    @Override
    75	    public Date getPubDate() {
    76	        Element pubDate = getElement(UPDATED);
    77	        return (pubDate != null) ? FeedUtils.convertAtomDate(pubDate.getContent()) : null;
    78	    }
    79	
    80	    @Override
    81	    public List<String> getCategories() {
    82	        List<Element> elementList = getElementList(CATEGORY);
    83	        
    84	        // Create list of category terms.
    85	        List<String> categories = new ArrayList<String>();
    86	        for (Element element : elementList) {
    87	            categories.add(element.getAttributes().getValue("term"));
    88	        }
    89	        
    90	        return categories;
    91	    }

    97	        List<Item> itemList = new ArrayList<Item>();
    98		    
    99	        // Build item list.
   100	        if (elementList != null) {
   101	            for (Element element : elementList) {
   102	                itemList.add((Item) element);
   103	            }
   104	        }
   105		    
   106		    return itemList;
   107		}
   108	    
   109	    @NonNull
   110	    @Override
   111	    public String toString() {
   112	        return getTitle();
   113	    }
   114	}

     2	
     3	import java.util.Stack;
     4	
     5	import org.apache.commons.text.StringEscapeUtils;
     6	import org.xml.sax.Attributes;
     7	import org.xml.sax.SAXException;
     8	import org.xml.sax.helpers.DefaultHandler;
     9	
    10	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Feed;
    11	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.FeedType;
    12	
    13	/**
    14	 * SAX parser content handler to process feed XML.
    15	 */
    16	class FeedHandler extends DefaultHandler {
    17	    private static final String RDF = "rdf";
    18	    private static final String RSS = "rss";
    19	    private static final String FEED = "feed";
    20	    private static final String ITEM = "item";
    21	    private static final String ENTRY = "entry";
    22	
    23	    private Stack<BaseElement> elementStack;
    24	    private Feed feed;
    25	    private FeedType type;
    26	    private StringBuilder buffer;
    27	    
    28	    /**
    29	     * Returns the feed.
    30	     */
    31	    public Feed getFeed() {
    32	        return feed;
    33	    }
    34	
    35	    @Override
    36	    public void characters(char[] ch, int start, int length) throws SAXException {
    37	        super.characters(ch, start, length);
    38	        buffer.append(ch, start, length);
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    @Override
    42	    public void startDocument() throws SAXException {
    43	        super.startDocument();
    44	        elementStack = new Stack<BaseElement>();
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    @Override
    48	    public void endDocument() throws SAXException {
    49	        elementStack.clear();
    50	        super.endDocument();
    51	    }
    52	    
    53	    @Override
    54	    public void startElement(String uri, String localName, String qName,
    55	            Attributes attributes) throws SAXException {
    56	        super.startElement(uri, localName, qName, attributes);
    57	        
    58	        BaseElement newElement;
    59	        
    60	        if (RDF.equalsIgnoreCase(localName)) {
    61	            // Create feed for RSS 1.0.
    62	            Rss1Feed newFeed = new Rss1Feed(uri, RDF, attributes);
    63	            feed = newFeed;
    64	            type = FeedType.RSS_1_0;
    65	            newElement = newFeed;
    66	            
    67	        } else if (RSS.equalsIgnoreCase(localName)) {
    68	            // Create feed for RSS 2.0.
    69	            Rss2Feed newFeed = new Rss2Feed(uri, RSS, attributes);
    70	            feed = newFeed;
    71	            type = FeedType.RSS_2_0;
    72	            newElement = newFeed;
    73	            
    74	        } else if (FEED.equalsIgnoreCase(localName)) {
    75	            // Create feed for Atom 1.0.
    76	            AtomFeed newFeed = new AtomFeed(uri, FEED, attributes);
    77	            feed = newFeed;
    78	            type = FeedType.ATOM_1_0;
    79	            newElement = newFeed;
    80	            
    81	        } else if (ITEM.equalsIgnoreCase(localName)) {
    82	            // Create RSS item.
    83	            switch (type) {
    84	            case RSS_1_0:
    85	                newElement = new Rss1Item(uri, localName, attributes);
    86	                break;
    87	            case RSS_2_0:
    88	                newElement = new Rss2Item(uri, localName, attributes);
    89	                break;
    90	            default:
    91	                throw new SAXException("Unknown feed type");
    92	            }
    93	
    94	        } else if (ENTRY.equalsIgnoreCase(localName)) {
    95	            // Create Atom item.
    96	            newElement = new AtomItem(uri, localName, attributes);
    97	
    98	        } else {
    99	            // Create new XML element.
   100	            newElement = new BaseElement(uri, localName, attributes);
   101	        }
   102	
   103	        elementStack.push(newElement);
   104	        
   105	        // Initialize content buffer.
   106	        buffer = new StringBuilder();
   107	    }
   108	
   109	    @Override
   110	    public void endElement(String uri, String localName, String qName)
   111	            throws SAXException {
   112	        super.endElement(uri, localName, qName);
   113	        
   114	        // Save content in current element.
   115	        BaseElement currentElement = elementStack.pop();
   116	        String content = buffer.toString();
   117	        // If contains ampersand, unescape escaped symbols
   118	        if (content.contains("&")) {
   119	            content = StringEscapeUtils.unescapeXml(content);
   120	        }
   121	        currentElement.setContent(content);
   122	        
   123	        // Add current element to its parent.
   124	        if (!elementStack.empty()) {
   125	            BaseElement parent = elementStack.peek();
   126	            parent.addElement(localName, currentElement);
   127	        }
   128	
   129	        // Clear content buffer.
   130	        buffer.delete(0, buffer.length());
   131	    }
   132	}
----- DefaultFeedParser.java 20-48
    20	
    21	    @Override
    22	    public Feed parse(InputStream inStream) throws FeedException {
    23	        try {
    24	            // Create SAX parser.
    25	            SAXParserFactory factory = SAXParserFactory.newInstance();
    26	            SAXParser parser = factory.newSAXParser();
    27	            
    28	            // Turn on namespace support.
    29	            XMLReader reader = parser.getXMLReader();
    30	            reader.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/namespaces", true);
    31	            reader.setFeature("http://xml.org/sax/features/namespace-prefixes", false);
    32	            
    33	        	// Create SAX handler.
    34	        	FeedHandler handler = new FeedHandler();
    35	        	
    36	            // Parse feed and return data.
    37	        	parser.parse(new XMLInputStream(inStream), handler);
    38	        	Feed feed = handler.getFeed();
    39	            if (feed == null)
    40	                throw new FeedException("Invalid RSS/Atom feed");
    41	            
    42	            return feed;
    43	        
    44	        } catch (Exception ex) {
    45	        	throw new FeedException(ex);
    46	        }
    47	    }
    48	}

     1	package com.ernieyu.feedparser;
     2	
     3	import java.util.List;
     4	import java.util.Set;
     5	
     6	import org.xml.sax.Attributes;
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * An XML element in the feed.
    10	 */
    11	public interface Element {
    12	
    13	    /**
    14	     * Returns the namespace URI.
    15	     */
    16	    public String getUri();
    17	
    18	    /**
    19	     * Returns the element name.
    20	     */
    21	    public String getName();
    22	    
    23	    /**
    24	     * Returns the attributes associated with the element.
    25	     */
    26	    public Attributes getAttributes();
    27	    
    28	    /**
    29	     * Returns the element content.
    30	     */
    31	    public String getContent();
    32	    
    33	    /**
    34	     * Returns the first child element associated with the specified name.
    35	     * Returns null if the element does not exist.
    36	     */
    37	    public Element getElement(String name);
    38	    
    39	    /**
    40	     * Returns a list of child elements associated with the specified name.
    41	     * Returns an empty list if no elements are available.
    42	     */
    43	    public List<Element> getElementList(String name);
    44	    
    45	    /**
    46	     * Returns a set of keys for all child elements.  This allows an 
    47	     * application to iterate through the elements.
    48	     */
    49	    public Set<String> getElementKeys();
    50	}

     8	/**
     9	 * An item in the feed.  This represents an RSS item or an Atom entry.
    10	 */
    11	public interface Item extends Element {
    12	
    13	    /**
    14	     * Returns the feed type.
    15	     */
    16	    public FeedType getType();
    17	    
    18	    /**
    19	     * Convenience method to retrieve the title.
    20	     */
    21	    public String getTitle();
    22	
    23	    /**
    24	     * Convenience method to retrieve the list of links.
    25	     */
    26	    public List<String> getLinks();
    27	
    28	    /**
    29	     * Convenience method to retrieve the description.
    30	     */
    31	    public String getDescription();
    32	
    33	    /**
    34	     * Convenience method to retrieve the author.
    35	     */
    36	    public String getAuthor();
    37	
    38	    /**
    39	     * Convenience method to retrieve the guid.
    40	     */
    41	    public String getGuid();
    42	
    43	    /**
    44	     * Convenience method to retrieve the published date.
    45	     */
    46	    public Date getPubDate();
    47	
    48	    /**
    49	     * Convenience method to retrieve a list of categories.
    50	     */
    51	    public List<String> getCategories();
    52	
    53	
    54	    /**
    55	     * Returns list of enclosures
    56	     */
    57	    public List<Enclosure> getEnclosures();
    58	
    59	    /**
    60	     * Returns the MediaRSS elements of this item, if supported, or null otherwise
    61	     */
    62	    public MediaRss getMediaRss();
    63	
    64	    /**
    65	     * Returns the EzRSS element of this item, if supported, or null otherwise
    66	     */
    67	    public EzRssTorrentItem getEzRssTorrentItem();
    68	    
    69	    /**
    70	     * Indicates whether the specified object is equal to this Item based on
    71	     * its unique identifier.
    72	     */
    73	    public boolean equals(Object obj);
    74	    
    75	    /**
    76	     * Returns a hash code based on the unique identifier referenced in the
    77	     * equals() method.
    78	     */
    79	    public int hashCode();
    80	}

     3	import java.util.ArrayList;
     4	import java.util.Collections;

    10	import org.xml.sax.Attributes;
    11	import org.xml.sax.helpers.AttributesImpl;
    12	
    13	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Element;
    14	
    15	/**
    16	 * Base class for XML elements.
    17	 */
    18	class BaseElement implements Element {
    19	
    20	    private final String uri;
    21	    private final String name;
    22	    private final Attributes attributes;
    23	    private final Map<String, List<Element>> elementMap = new HashMap<String, List<Element>>();
    24	    private String content;
    25	    
    26	    /**
    27	     * Constructs an Element with the specified namespace uri and name.
    28	     */
    29	    public BaseElement(String uri, String name) {
    30	        this.uri = uri;
    31	        this.name = name;
    32	        this.attributes = new AttributesImpl();
    33	    }
    34	    
    35	    /**
    36	     * Constructs an Element with the specified namespace uri, name and 
    37	     * attributes.
    38	     */
    39	    public BaseElement(String uri, String name, Attributes attributes) {
    40	        this.uri = uri;
    41	        this.name = name;
    42	        this.attributes = new AttributesImpl(attributes);
    43	    }
    44	    
    45	    @Override
    46	    public String getUri() {
    47	        return uri;
    48	    }
    49	    
    50	    @Override
    51	    public String getName() {
    52	        return name;
    53	    }
    54	    
    55	    @Override
    56	    public Attributes getAttributes() {
    57	        return attributes;
    58	    }
    59	    
    60	    @Override
    61	    public String getContent() {
    62	        return content;
    63	    }
    64	    
    65	    /**
    66	     * Sets the element content.
    67	     */
    68	    void setContent(String data) {
    69	        this.content = data;
    70	    }
    71	    
    72	    @Override
    73	    public Element getElement(String name) {
    74	        List<Element> elements = elementMap.get(name);
    75	        return (elements != null) ? elements.get(0) : null;
    76	    }
    77	    
    78	    @Override
    79	    public List<Element> getElementList(String name) {
    80	        return elementMap.getOrDefault(name, Collections.emptyList());
    81	    }
    82	
    83	    @Override
    84	    public Set<String> getElementKeys() {
    85	        return elementMap.keySet();
    86	    }
    87	    
    88	    /**
    89	     * Adds the specified child element to the element.
    90	     */
    91	    void addElement(String name, Element element) {
    92	        if (elementMap.containsKey(name)) {
    93	            elementMap.get(name).add(element);
    94	        } else {
    95	            List<Element> newList = new ArrayList<Element>();
    96	            newList.add(element);
    97	            elementMap.put(name, newList);
    98	        }
    99	    }
   100	}
----- BaseItem.java
     1	package com.ernieyu.feedparser.impl;
     2	
     3	import androidx.annotation.NonNull;
     4	
     5	import org.xml.sax.Attributes;
     6	
     7	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.FeedUtils;
     8	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Item;
     9	
    10	import java.util.List;
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * Base class for feed items.
    14	 */
    15	abstract class BaseItem extends BaseElement implements Item {
    16	
    17	    /**
    18	     * Constructs a BaseItem with the specified namespace uri, name and 
    19	     * attributes.
    20	     */
    21	    public BaseItem(String uri, String name, Attributes attributes) {
    22	        super(uri, name, attributes);
    23	    }
    24	    
    25	    /**
    26	     * Returns the unique identifier for item equality.  This is usually based
    27	     * on the Guid value.  This method uses alternate values when the Guid 
    28	     * value is missing.
    29	     */
    30	    private String getUniqueId() {
    31	        String id = getGuid();
    32	        if (id == null) {
    33	            List<String> links = getLinks();
    34	            if (links.size() == 1)
    35	                id = links.get(0);
    36	        }
    37	        if (id == null) {
    38	            id = getTitle();
    39	        }
    40	        if (id == null) {
    41	            id = getDescription();
    42	        }
    43	        return id;
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    @NonNull
    47	    @Override
    48	    public String toString() {
    49	        return getTitle();
    50	    }
    51	    
    52	    @Override
    53	    public boolean equals(Object obj) {
    54	        // Compare ids for equality.
    55	        if (obj instanceof BaseItem) {
    56	            String id1 = getUniqueId();
    57	            String id2 = ((BaseItem) obj).getUniqueId();
    58	            return FeedUtils.equalsOrNull(id1, id2);
    59	        }
    60	        
    61	        return false;
    62	    }
    63	    
    64	    @Override
    65	    public int hashCode() {
    66	        // Use same fields as equals method. 
    67	        String id = getUniqueId();
    68	        return (id != null) ? id.hashCode() : 0;
    69	    }
    70	}

    11	/**
    12	 * Item implementation for Atom feeds.
    13	 */
    14	class AtomItem extends BaseItem {
    15	    // XML elements for Atom items.
    16	    private static final String TITLE = "title";
    17	    private static final String LINK = "link";
    18	    private static final String UPDATED = "updated";
    19	    private static final String ID = "id";
    20	    private static final String CONTENT = "content";
    21	    private static final String SUMMARY = "summary";
    22	    private static final String AUTHOR = "author";
    23	    private static final String NAME = "name";
    24	    private static final String CATEGORY = "category";
    25	    
    26	    /**
    27	     * Constructs an AtomItem with the specified namespace uri, name and 
    28	     * attributes.
    29	     */
    30	    public AtomItem(String uri, String name, Attributes attributes) {
    31	        super(uri, name, attributes);
    32	    }
    33	    
    34	    @Override
    35	    public FeedType getType() {
    36	        return FeedType.ATOM_1_0;
    37	    }
    38	    
    39	    @Override
    40	    public String getTitle() {
    41	        Element title = getElement(TITLE);
    42	        return (title != null) ? title.getContent() : null;
    43	    }
    44	
    45	    @Override
    46	    public List<String> getLinks() {
    47	        ArrayList<String> links = new ArrayList<String>();
    48	        List<Element> elements = getElementList(LINK);
    49	        for (Element element : elements) {
    50	            links.add(element.getAttributes().getValue("href"));
    51	        }
    52	
    53	        return links;
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    @Override
    57	    public String getDescription() {
    58	        Element descr = getElement(CONTENT);
    59	        if (descr == null) descr = getElement(SUMMARY);
    60	        return (descr != null) ? descr.getContent() : null;
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    @Override
    75	    public List<String> getCategories() {
    76	        List<Element> elementList = getElementList(CATEGORY);
    77	        
    78	        // Create list of category terms.
    79	        List<String> categories = new ArrayList<String>();
    80	        for (Element element : elementList) {
    81	            categories.add(element.getAttributes().getValue("term"));
    82	        }
    83	        
    84	        return categories;
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    @Override
    88	    public List<Enclosure> getEnclosures() {
    89	        ArrayList<Enclosure> enclosures = new ArrayList<Enclosure>();
    90	        List<Element> links = getElementList(LINK);
    91	
    92	        for (Element link : links) {
    93	            Attributes attr = link.getAttributes();
    94	            String rel = attr.getValue("rel");
    95	            if (rel == null || !rel.equalsIgnoreCase("enclosure"))
    96	                continue;
    97	
    98	            String url = attr.getValue("href");
    99	            String type = attr.getValue("type");
   100	            String lengthStr = attr.getValue("length");
   101	            long length = 0;
   102	            if (lengthStr != null)
   103	                length = Long.parseLong(lengthStr);
   104	
   105	            enclosures.add(new Enclosure(url, type, length));
   106	        }
   107	
   108	        return enclosures;
   109	    }

   112	     * Not supported
   113	     */

   117	        return null;
   118	    }

   122	     */
   123	
   124	    @Override
   125	    public EzRssTorrentItem getEzRssTorrentItem() {
   126	        return null;
   127	    }

     1	package com.ernieyu.feedparser.impl;
     2	
     3	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Element;
     4	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.EzRssTorrentItem;
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * A parser for EzRSS 0.1 namespace (http://xmlns.ezrss.it/0.1)
     8	 */
     9	
    10	class EzRss01Parser {
    11	    private static final String EZRSS_NAMESPACE_URI = "http://xmlns.ezrss.it/0.1";
    12	    private static final String EZRSS_FILE_NAME = "fileName";
    13	    private static final String EZRSS_MAGNET_URI = "magnetURI";
    14	    private static final String EZRSS_INFO_HASH = "infoHash";
    15	    private static final String EZRSS_CONTENT_LENGTH = "contentLength";
    16	    private static final String EZRSS_SEEDS = "seeds";
    17	    private static final String EZRSS_PEERS = "peers";
    18	    private static final String EZRSS_VERIFIED = "verified";
    19	
    20	    private Rss2Item item;
    21	
    22	    public EzRss01Parser(Rss2Item item) {
    23	        this.item = item;
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    public EzRssTorrentItem parse() {
    27	        String fileName = parseFileName();
    28	        String magnetUri = parseMagnetUri();
    29	        String infoHash = parseInfoHash();
    30	        long contentLength = parseContentLength();
    31	        int seeds = parseSeeds();
    32	        int peers = parsePeers();
    33	        boolean verified = parseVerified();
    34	
    35	        return new EzRssTorrentItem(fileName, magnetUri, infoHash,
    36	            contentLength, seeds, peers, verified);
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    private String parseFileName() {
    40	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_FILE_NAME);
    41	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    42	            return null;
    43	
    44	        return element.getContent();
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    private String parseMagnetUri() {
    48	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_MAGNET_URI);
    49	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    50	            return null;
    51	
    52	        return element.getContent();
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    private String parseInfoHash() {
    56	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_INFO_HASH);
    57	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    58	            return null;
    59	
    60	        return element.getContent();
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    private long parseContentLength() {
    64	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_CONTENT_LENGTH);
    65	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    66	            return 0;
    67	
    68	        long contentLength = 0;
    69	        try {
    70	            contentLength = Long.parseLong(element.getContent());
    71	
    72	        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
    73	            return contentLength;
    74	        }
    75	
    76	        return contentLength;
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    private int parseSeeds() {
    80	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_SEEDS);
    81	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    82	            return 0;
    83	
    84	        return parseInt(element.getContent());
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    private int parsePeers() {
    88	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_PEERS);
    89	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    90	            return 0;
    91	
    92	        return parseInt(element.getContent());
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    private boolean parseVerified() {
    96	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_VERIFIED);
    97	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    98	            return false;
    99	
   100	        int verifiedVal = parseInt(element.getContent());
   101	
   102	        return verifiedVal == 1;
   103	    }
   104	
   105	    private int parseInt(String s)
   106	    {
   107	        int i = 0;
   108	        try {
   109	            i = Integer.parseInt(s);
   110	
   111	        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
   112	            return i;
   113	        }
   114	
   115	        return i;
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    private boolean isEzRssElement(Element element) {
   119	        return element.getUri() != null &&
   120	            element.getUri().startsWith(EZRSS_NAMESPACE_URI);
   121	    }
   122	}
----- Rss2Item.java 111-122
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    @Override
   114	    public MediaRss getMediaRss() {
   115	       return new MediaRssParser(this).parse();
   116	    }
   117	
   118	    @Override
   119	    public EzRssTorrentItem getEzRssTorrentItem() {
   120	        return new EzRss01Parser(this).parse();
   121	    }
   122	}

    89	    public List<FeedItem> getItems()
    90	    {
    91	        List<FeedItem> items = new ArrayList<>();
    92	        if (feed == null)
    93	            return items;
    94	
    95	        for (Item item : feed.getItemList()) {
    96	            List<String> links = item.getLinks();
    97	            String articleUrl = getFirstNotNullLink(links);
    98	            String downloadUrl = watchDownloadableLink(links);
    99	
   100	            /* Find url with torrent/magnet */
   101	            if (downloadUrl == null)
   102	                downloadUrl = findDownloadUrl(item);
   103	
   104	            Date pubDate = item.getPubDate();
   105	            long pubDateTime = 0;
   106	            if (pubDate != null)
   107	                pubDateTime = pubDate.getTime();
   108	
   109	            FeedItem feedItem = new FeedItem(feedChannel.id, downloadUrl,
   110	                    articleUrl, item.getTitle(), pubDateTime);
   111	            feedItem.fetchDate = System.currentTimeMillis();
----- lines 141,161
   141	    private String findDownloadUrl(Item item)
   142	    {
   143	        /*
   144	         * Watch tags in descending order of importance
   145	         * (hashes in the last place)
   146	         */
   147	        String enclosureUrl = watchEnclosure(item);
   148	        if (enclosureUrl != null)
   149	            return enclosureUrl;
   150	
   151	        String infoHash = watchTorrentInfoHash(item);
   152	        if (infoHash != null)
   153	            return infoHash;
   154	
   155	        String mediaContentUrl = watchMediaContent(item);
   156	        if (mediaContentUrl != null)
   157	            return mediaContentUrl;
   158	
   159	        String guid = watchGuid(item);
   160	        return guid;
   161	    }
----- lines 186,208
   186	     */
   187	
   188	    private String watchMediaContent(Item item)
   189	    {
   190	        MediaRss media = item.getMediaRss();
   191	        if (media == null)
   192	            return null;
   193	
   194	        List<Content> contentList = media.getContent();
   195	        for (Content content : contentList) {
   196	            if (content == null)
   197	                continue;
   198	
   199	            String url = content.getUrl();
   200	            if (url == null)
   201	                return watchMediaHash(media);
   202	            String type = content.getType();
   203	            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
   204	                return url;
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        return watchMediaHash(media);
   208	    }
----- lines 236,262
   236	    private String watchTorrentInfoHash(Item item)
   237	    {
   238	        EzRssTorrentItem torrentItem = item.getEzRssTorrentItem();
   239	        if (torrentItem == null)
   240	            return null;
   241	
   242	        String infoHash = torrentItem.getInfoHash();
   243	        if (infoHash != null && Utils.isHash(infoHash))
   244	            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(infoHash);
   245	
   246	        return null;
   247	    }
   248	
   249	    /*
   250	     * Parse element like this:
   251	     * <guid>http://foo.com/bar.torrent</guid>
   252	     */
   253	
   254	    private String watchGuid(Item item)
   255	    {
   256	        String url = item.getGuid();
   257	        if (url != null && isMagnetOrTorrent(url))
   258	            return url;
   259	
   260	        return null;
   261	    }

   204	
   205	def add_auto_edges(gdir, edges, e, suppress):
   206	    auto = json.load(open(os.path.join(gdir, 'auto_calls.json')))
   207	    manual = {(base(x['src']), base(x['dst'])) for x in edges if x['kind'] != 'REGISTER'}
   208	    manual_reg = {(base(x['src']), base(x['dst'])) for x in edges if x['kind'] == 'REGISTER'}
   209	    receiver = set(auto.get('receiver_variants', ()))
   210	    variants = collections.defaultdict(set)  # hand-written context variants (not receiver variants)
   211	    for x in edges:
   212	        for n in (x['src'], x['dst']):
   213	            if '@' in n and not is_screen(n) and n not in receiver:
   214	                variants[base(n)].add(n)
   215	    manual_nodes = {(x['src'], base(x['dst'])) for x in edges if x['kind'] != 'REGISTER'}
   216	    for c in auto['calls']:
   217	        p = (c['src'], c['dst'])
   218	        if p in suppress or c['src'] in suppress or c['dst'] in suppress:
   219	            continue
   220	        if c['src'] in receiver:  # receiver variants: skip only if that exact node already has a hand edge
   221	            if (c['src'], base(c['dst'])) in manual_nodes:
   222	                continue
   223	        elif (c['src'], base(c['dst'])) in manual or (base(c['src']), base(c['dst'])) in manual:
   224	            continue
   225	        trig = 'Direct call' if c['dispatch'] == 'exact' else 'Virtual/interface call that can dispatch to this implementation'
   226	        if c['via']:
   227	            trig += ' (through ' + ', '.join(c['via']) + ')'
   228	        for s in [c['src']] + sorted(variants.get(c['src'], ())):
   229	            e(s, c['dst'], 'CALL', trig, evidence='SRC %s:%s (bytecode)' % (c['file'], c['line']), auto=True)
   230	    for l in auto['lambdas']:
   231	        p = (l['src'], l['dst'])
   232	        if p in manual or p in manual_reg or p in suppress or l['src'] in suppress:
   233	            continue
   234	        e(l['src'], l['dst'], 'REGISTER', 'Creates the lambda / method reference (its body runs when invoked)',
   235	          evidence='SRC %s:%s (bytecode)' % (l['file'], l['line']), auto=True)

    62	    public FeedParser(@NonNull Context context, @NonNull FeedChannel feedChannel) throws Exception
    63	    {
    64	        this.feedChannel = feedChannel;
    65	        ByteArrayInputStream bsStream = null;
    66	        try {
    67	            byte[] response = Utils.fetchHttpUrl(context, feedChannel.url);
    68	            if (response == null)
    69	                return;
    70	            bsStream = new ByteArrayInputStream(response);
    71	            feed = FeedParserFactory.newParser().parse(bsStream);
    72	
    73	        } finally {
    74	            if (bsStream != null) {
    75	                try {
    76	                    bsStream.close();
    77	                } catch (Exception e) {
    78	                    /* Ignore */
    79	                }
    80	            }
    81	        }

   141	    private String findDownloadUrl(Item item)
   142	    {
   143	        /*
   144	         * Watch tags in descending order of importance

   156	        if (mediaContentUrl != null)

   188	    private String watchMediaContent(Item item)
   189	    {
   190	        MediaRss media = item.getMediaRss();
   191	        if (media == null)
   192	            return null;
   193	
   194	        List<Content> contentList = media.getContent();
   195	        for (Content content : contentList) {
   196	            if (content == null)
   197	                continue;
   198	
   199	            String url = content.getUrl();
   200	            if (url == null)
   201	                return watchMediaHash(media);
   202	            String type = content.getType();
   203	            if (isMagnetOrTorrent(url) || (type != null && type.equals(Utils.MIME_TORRENT)))
   204	                return url;
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        return watchMediaHash(media);
   208	    }
   209	
   234	     */
   235	
   236	    private String watchTorrentInfoHash(Item item)
   237	    {
   238	        EzRssTorrentItem torrentItem = item.getEzRssTorrentItem();
   239	        if (torrentItem == null)
   240	            return null;
   241	
   242	        String infoHash = torrentItem.getInfoHash();
   243	        if (infoHash != null && Utils.isHash(infoHash))
   244	            return Utils.normalizeMagnetHash(infoHash);
   245	
   246	        return null;
   247	    }

   258	            return url;
   259	
   260	        return null;

    16	 * Base class for XML elements.
    17	 */
    18	class BaseElement implements Element {
    19	
    20	    private final String uri;
    21	    private final String name;
    22	    private final Attributes attributes;
    23	    private final Map<String, List<Element>> elementMap = new HashMap<String, List<Element>>();
    24	    private String content;
    25	    
    26	    /**
    27	     * Constructs an Element with the specified namespace uri and name.
    28	     */
    29	    public BaseElement(String uri, String name) {
    30	        this.uri = uri;
    31	        this.name = name;
    32	        this.attributes = new AttributesImpl();
    33	    }
    34	    
    35	    /**
    36	     * Constructs an Element with the specified namespace uri, name and 
    37	     * attributes.
    38	     */
    39	    public BaseElement(String uri, String name, Attributes attributes) {
    40	        this.uri = uri;
    41	        this.name = name;
    42	        this.attributes = new AttributesImpl(attributes);
    43	    }
    44	    
    45	    @Override
    46	    public String getUri() {
    47	        return uri;
    48	    }
    49	    
    50	    @Override
    51	    public String getName() {
    52	        return name;
    53	    }
    54	    
    55	    @Override
    56	    public Attributes getAttributes() {
    57	        return attributes;
    58	    }
    59	    
    60	    @Override
    61	    public String getContent() {
    62	        return content;
    63	    }
    64	    
    65	    /**
    66	     * Sets the element content.
    67	     */
    68	    void setContent(String data) {
    69	        this.content = data;
    70	    }
    71	    
    72	    @Override
    73	    public Element getElement(String name) {
    74	        List<Element> elements = elementMap.get(name);
    75	        return (elements != null) ? elements.get(0) : null;
    76	    }
    77	    
    78	    @Override
    79	    public List<Element> getElementList(String name) {
    80	        return elementMap.getOrDefault(name, Collections.emptyList());
    81	    }
    82	
    83	    @Override
    84	    public Set<String> getElementKeys() {
    85	        return elementMap.keySet();
    86	    }
    87	    
    88	    /**
    89	     * Adds the specified child element to the element.
    90	     */
    91	    void addElement(String name, Element element) {
    92	        if (elementMap.containsKey(name)) {
    93	            elementMap.get(name).add(element);
    94	        } else {
    95	            List<Element> newList = new ArrayList<Element>();
    96	            newList.add(element);
    97	            elementMap.put(name, newList);
    98	        }
    99	    }
   100	}

     1	package com.ernieyu.feedparser.impl;
     2	
     3	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.Element;
     4	import com.ernieyu.feedparser.EzRssTorrentItem;
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * A parser for EzRSS 0.1 namespace (http://xmlns.ezrss.it/0.1)
     8	 */
     9	
    10	class EzRss01Parser {
    11	    private static final String EZRSS_NAMESPACE_URI = "http://xmlns.ezrss.it/0.1";
    12	    private static final String EZRSS_FILE_NAME = "fileName";
    13	    private static final String EZRSS_MAGNET_URI = "magnetURI";
    14	    private static final String EZRSS_INFO_HASH = "infoHash";
    15	    private static final String EZRSS_CONTENT_LENGTH = "contentLength";
    16	    private static final String EZRSS_SEEDS = "seeds";
    17	    private static final String EZRSS_PEERS = "peers";
    18	    private static final String EZRSS_VERIFIED = "verified";
    19	
    20	    private Rss2Item item;
    21	
    22	    public EzRss01Parser(Rss2Item item) {
    23	        this.item = item;
    24	    }
    25	
    26	    public EzRssTorrentItem parse() {
    27	        String fileName = parseFileName();
    28	        String magnetUri = parseMagnetUri();
    29	        String infoHash = parseInfoHash();
    30	        long contentLength = parseContentLength();
    31	        int seeds = parseSeeds();
    32	        int peers = parsePeers();
    33	        boolean verified = parseVerified();
    34	
    35	        return new EzRssTorrentItem(fileName, magnetUri, infoHash,
    36	            contentLength, seeds, peers, verified);
    37	    }
    38	
    39	    private String parseFileName() {
    40	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_FILE_NAME);
    41	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    42	            return null;
    43	
    44	        return element.getContent();
    45	    }

    47	    private String parseMagnetUri() {
    48	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_MAGNET_URI);
    49	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    50	            return null;
    51	
    52	        return element.getContent();
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    private String parseInfoHash() {
    56	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_INFO_HASH);
    57	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    58	            return null;
    59	
    60	        return element.getContent();
    61	    }
    62	
    63	    private long parseContentLength() {
    64	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_CONTENT_LENGTH);
    65	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    66	            return 0;
    67	
    68	        long contentLength = 0;
    69	        try {
    70	            contentLength = Long.parseLong(element.getContent());
    71	
    72	        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
    73	            return contentLength;
    74	        }
    75	
    76	        return contentLength;
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    private int parseSeeds() {
    80	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_SEEDS);
    81	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    82	            return 0;
    83	
    84	        return parseInt(element.getContent());
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    private int parsePeers() {
    88	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_PEERS);
    89	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    90	            return 0;
    91	
    92	        return parseInt(element.getContent());
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    private boolean parseVerified() {
    96	        Element element = item.getElement(EZRSS_VERIFIED);
    97	        if (element == null || !isEzRssElement(element))
    98	            return false;
    99	
   100	        int verifiedVal = parseInt(element.getContent());
   101	
   102	        return verifiedVal == 1;
   103	    }

   105	    private int parseInt(String s)
   106	    {
   107	        int i = 0;
   108	        try {
   109	            i = Integer.parseInt(s);
   110	
   111	        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
   112	            return i;
   113	        }
   114	
   115	        return i;
   116	    }

   119	        return element.getUri() != null &&
   120	            element.getUri().startsWith(EZRSS_NAMESPACE_URI);
   121	    }

   101	        }

   104

   108	
   109	    @Override
   110	    public void endElement(String uri, String localName, String qName)
   111	            throws SAXException {
   112	        super.endElement(uri, localName, qName);
   113	        
   114	        // Save content in current element.
   115	        BaseElement currentElement = elementStack.pop();
   116	        String content = buffer.toString();
   117	        // If contains ampersand, unescape escaped symbols
   118	        if (content.contains("&")) {
   119	            content = StringEscapeUtils.unescapeXml(content);
   120	        }
   121	        currentElement.setContent(content);
   122	        
   123	        // Add current element to its parent.
   124	        if (!elementStack.empty()) {
   125	            BaseElement parent = elementStack.peek();
   126	            parent.addElement(localName, currentElement);
   127	        }
   128	
   129	        // Clear content buffer.
   130	        buffer.delete(0, buffer.length());
   131	    }

$ nl -ba tools/build_graph.py 204-236 (read only; build not run)
   204	
   205	def add_auto_edges(gdir, edges, e, suppress):
   206	    auto = json.load(open(os.path.join(gdir, 'auto_calls.json')))
   207	    manual = {(base(x['src']), base(x['dst'])) for x in edges if x['kind'] != 'REGISTER'}
   208	    manual_reg = {(base(x['src']), base(x['dst'])) for x in edges if x['kind'] == 'REGISTER'}
   209	    receiver = set(auto.get('receiver_variants', ()))
   210	    variants = collections.defaultdict(set)  # hand-written context variants (not receiver variants)
   211	    for x in edges:
   212	        for n in (x['src'], x['dst']):
   213	            if '@' in n and not is_screen(n) and n not in receiver:
   214	                variants[base(n)].add(n)
   215	    manual_nodes = {(x['src'], base(x['dst'])) for x in edges if x['kind'] != 'REGISTER'}
   216	    for c in auto['calls']:
   217	        p = (c['src'], c['dst'])
   218	        if p in suppress or c['src'] in suppress or c['dst'] in suppress:
   219	            continue
   220	        if c['src'] in receiver:  # receiver variants: skip only if that exact node already has a hand edge
   221	            if (c['src'], base(c['dst'])) in manual_nodes:
   222	                continue
   223	        elif (c['src'], base(c['dst'])) in manual or (base(c['src']), base(c['dst'])) in manual:
   224	            continue
   225	        trig = 'Direct call' if c['dispatch'] == 'exact' else 'Virtual/interface call that can dispatch to this implementation'
   226	        if c['via']:
   227	            trig += ' (through ' + ', '.join(c['via']) + ')'
   228	        for s in [c['src']] + sorted(variants.get(c['src'], ())):
   229	            e(s, c['dst'], 'CALL', trig, evidence='SRC %s:%s (bytecode)' % (c['file'], c['line']), auto=True)
   230	    for l in auto['lambdas']:
   231	        p = (l['src'], l['dst'])
   232	        if p in manual or p in manual_reg or p in suppress or l['src'] in suppress:
   233	            continue
   234	        e(l['src'], l['dst'], 'REGISTER', 'Creates the lambda / method reference (its body runs when invoked)',
   235	          evidence='SRC %s:%s (bytecode)' % (l['file'], l['line']), auto=True)
   236

    54	    public void startElement(String uri, String localName, String qName,
    55	            Attributes attributes) throws SAXException {

    57	        
    58	        BaseElement newElement;

    61	            // Create feed for RSS 1.0.
    62	            Rss1Feed newFeed = new Rss1Feed(uri, RDF, attributes);
    63	            feed = newFeed;

    66

    68	            // Create feed for RSS 2.0.
    69	            Rss2Feed newFeed = new Rss2Feed(uri, RSS, attributes);
    70	            feed = newFeed;
    71	            type = FeedType.RSS_2_0;
    72	            newElement = newFeed;

    75	            // Create feed for Atom 1.0.
    76	            AtomFeed newFeed = new AtomFeed(uri, FEED, attributes);
    77	            feed = newFeed;
    78	            type = FeedType.ATOM_1_0;
    79	            newElement = newFeed;
    80	            
    81	        } else if (ITEM.equalsIgnoreCase(localName)) {
    82	            // Create RSS item.
    83	            switch (type) {
    84	            case RSS_1_0:
    85	                newElement = new Rss1Item(uri, localName, attributes);
    86	                break;
    87	            case RSS_2_0:
    88	                newElement = new Rss2Item(uri, localName, attributes);
    89	                break;
    90	            default:
    91	                throw new SAXException("Unknown feed type");
    92	            }

$ nl -ba impl/BaseItem.java 24-69
    24	    
    25	    /**
    26	     * Returns the unique identifier for item equality.  This is usually based
    27	     * on the Guid value.  This method uses alternate values when the Guid 
    28	     * value is missing.
    29	     */
    30	    private String getUniqueId() {
    31	        String id = getGuid();
    32	        if (id == null) {
    33	            List<String> links = getLinks();
    34	            if (links.size() == 1)
    35	                id = links.get(0);
    36	        }
    37	        if (id == null) {
    38	            id = getTitle();
    39	        }
    40	        if (id == null) {
    41	            id = getDescription();
    42	        }
    43	        return id;
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    @NonNull
    47	    @Override
    48	    public String toString() {
    49	        return getTitle();
    50	    }
    51	    
    52	    @Override
    53	    public boolean equals(Object obj) {
    54	        // Compare ids for equality.
    55	        if (obj instanceof BaseItem) {
    56	            String id1 = getUniqueId();
    57	            String id2 = ((BaseItem) obj).getUniqueId();
    58	            return FeedUtils.equalsOrNull(id1, id2);
    59	        }
    60	        
    61	        return false;
    62	    }
    63	    
    64	    @Override
    65	    public int hashCode() {
    66	        // Use same fields as equals method. 
    67	        String id = getUniqueId();
    68	        return (id != null) ? id.hashCode() : 0;
    69	    }
